### [1] [Dynamical Stability and Quasinormal Modes of Casimir Wormholes in dRGT Massive Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06893)
*Piyali Bhar,Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,A. Errehymy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在dRGT大质量引力框架下，由Casimir能量支撑的静态球对称可穿越虫洞，推导出精确的喉部形状函数，验证了Morris-Thorne条件；Casimir能密度导致全程违反零能量条件（NEC），且NEC违背程度受大质量引力参数 $m_g^2 c_2$ 调控；系统满足广义TOV力学平衡；标量扰动的准正规模谱显示有效势垒高度与多极数ℓ及质量引力参数正相关，阻尼率|ω_I|随 $m_g^2 c_2$ 增大而增强，为引力波观测中区分Casimir虫洞与黑洞提供了理论标识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建物理上自洽、非渐近平直但可穿越的虫洞几何，探索量子真空效应（Casimir能量）与修改引力（dRGT massive gravity）协同支撑虫洞的可能性，并寻求可观测的引力波特征信号。

Method: 解析求解dRGT massive gravity中Einstein方程与Casimir应力-能量张量耦合系统，获得精确形状函数b(r)；应用Morris-Thorne喉部条件、NEC检验、体积积分量化器（VIQ）、广义Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程分析力学平衡；采用Mashhoon解析法与数值积分联合求解标量扰动的薛定谔类波动方程，提取准正规模（QNM）频谱。

Result: 1) 得到含Casimir项与dRGT质量项的精确形状函数，满足b(r₀)=r₀及b′(r₀)<1；2) Casimir能密度ρ=−π²/(720r⁴)导致(ρ+pᵣ)<0与(ρ−|pᵣ|)<0全域成立，NEC严格违反；3) VIQ表明∫(ρ+pᵣ)dV随m_g²c₂增大而更负；4) 广义TOV方程严格满足各向异性力学平衡；5) QNM谱显示有效势垒对称于喉部，其峰值与ℓ和m_g²c₂正相关，|ω_I|随m_g²c₂单调递增。

Conclusion: Casimir能量可在dRGT massive gravity中稳定支撑静态球对称可穿越虫洞，其非渐近平直几何与特征性准正规模谱（尤其是阻尼率对m_g²c₂的敏感依赖）构成区别于黑洞的可观测引力波指纹，为未来LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及第三代探测器提供理论判据。

Abstract: This paper investigates static, spherically symmetric traversable wormholes supported by Casimir energy within de Rham-Gabadadze-Tolley (dRGT) massive gravity. By deriving the exact shape function $b(r) = r_0 + \frac{π^3(-r + r_0)}{90 r r_0} - \frac{1}{2} C m_g^2 (r - r_0)[2C c_2 + c_1(r + r_0)]$, we verify that the geometry satisfies the local Morris--Thorne throat and traversability conditions, including $b(r_0)=r_0$ and $b'(r_0)<1$. For the nonzero massive-gravity parameters considered here, the resulting spacetime is not asymptotically flat, and its large-$r$ behavior is governed by the massive-gravity contributions to the shape function. The Casimir energy density $ρ= -π^2/(720 r^4)$ ensures the Null Energy Condition is violated throughout the throat region, as demonstrated by the negative behavior of $(ρ+ p_r)$ and $(ρ- |p_r|)$ across all parameter values. Analysis of the volume integral quantifier reveals that the massive gravity parameter $m_g^2 c_2$ directly regulates the exotic matter requirement, with the integrated NEC violation becoming more negative as $m_g^2 c_2$ increases from $0.1$ to $0.5$. The mechanical equilibrium is rigorously established via the generalized Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation, where the anisotropic force precisely balances the gravitational and hydrostatic forces. The quasinormal mode spectrum for scalar perturbations is extracted using both the analytical Mashhoon method and direct numerical integration. The effective potential forms a symmetric barrier at the throat, with the peak height increasing with the multipole number $\ell$. The damping rate $|ω_I|$ grows systematically with increasing massive gravity parameter. These distinct spectral signatures provide robust theoretical markers for distinguishing Casimir wormholes from black holes in future gravitational wave observations.

</details>


### [2] [The pseudospectrum for gravitational perturbations in Kerr spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06954)
*Hongsheng Zhang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr黑洞在自旋权重s=2（引力波天文学中最相关情形）下的伪谱性质，采用双维Chebyshev配置的双曲面框架，将准正规模问题表述为非自伴本征值问题；利用Teukolsky–Starobinsky恒等式构造H²能量范数以克服s≠0时单场能量范数缺失的问题；发现伪谱随范数提升（L²→H¹→H²）显著收紧但仍保持较大尺度；s=±2算子虽等谱但不等伪谱；引力扰动的伪谱远强于标量扰动，表明Kerr黑洞的QNM谱不稳定是普适且在引力情形下更强的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Kerr黑洞准正规模（QNM）的谱稳定性——即伪谱行为——对引力波建模、参数估计及数值相对论中的模式提取至关重要；尤其s=2引力扰动缺乏守恒流定义的单场能量范数，亟需构造适配的高阶能量范数以量化谱不稳定性。

Method: 采用双曲面坐标系与二维Chebyshev谱方法离散化Teukolsky方程；利用Teukolsky–Starobinsky恒等式将Ψ^{(-2)}表示为Ψ^{(2)}的四阶微分算子作用结果，并经对称化导出H²能量范数；构建ω-无关的双场层次结构；系统比较L²、H¹、H²范数下的伪谱半径与预解式范数，对比s=0与s=±2情形。

Result: 1) 伪谱半径随范数阶数提升（L²→H¹→H²）收紧1.1–3.5个数量级，但始终显著非零；2) s=+2与s=−2算子等谱但不等伪谱：在14个探测点中12个点ψ₀预解式H²范数比ψ₄大0.19–0.82个数量级，最远离QNM线的两点则反向差0.18–0.19；3) 引力（s=2）伪谱在窗口最小值处比标量（s=0）高0.5–5.2个数量级，在L²范数下于ωM=0.47−0.17i处高出5.9个数量级；4) s=2预解式沿衰减方向呈非单调，而s=0光滑单调。

Conclusion: Kerr黑洞的准正规模谱不稳定（即伪谱显著非平凡）是其普遍物理属性，且在引力扰动（s=2）下比标量扰动（s=0）更强；该不稳定性不能被任何ω-无关的单场或双场能量范数完全压制，凸显了高自旋扰动在频域分析中的内在病态性。

Abstract: We study the pseudospectrum of the Kerr black hole for gravitational perturbations with spin weight $s=2$, the case of greatest interest in gravitational-wave astronomy. In the hyperboloidal framework with two-dimensional Chebyshev collocation, we formulate the quasinormal mode problem as a non-self-adjoint eigenvalue problem. For $s\neq0$ the fields $Ψ^{(s)}$ and $Ψ^{(-s)}$ are independent, so the conserved current method yields no single-field energy norm. We overcome this with the Teukolsky--Starobinsky identities, which relate $Ψ^{(-2)}$ to a fourth-order operator on $Ψ^{(2)}$ and yield an $H^2$ energy norm after symmetrization. With the $H^1$ norm known for $s=0$, this suggests the pattern $H^{|s|}$, verified for $s=0$ and $s=2$. All results use an $ω$-independent two-field hierarchy. We find that the pseudospectrum tightens by $1.1$ to $3.5$ decades at each step of $L^2\to H^1\to H^2$, yet stays large in every norm. The $s=+2$ and $s=-2$ operators are isospectral but not isopseudospectral: at twelve of fourteen probes the $ψ_0$ resolvent exceeds the $ψ_4$ one by $0.19$ to $0.82$ decades in the $H^2$ norm, while at the two probes furthest along the QNM line the ordering reverses by $0.18$ to $0.19$ decades. The gravitational pseudospectrum exceeds the scalar one by $0.5$ to $5.2$ decades in the window minimum and by $5.9$ decades in $L^2$ at $ωM=0.47-0.17\,i$, while along the damping direction the $s=2$ resolvent is non-monotonic and the $s=0$ resolvent smooth. QNM spectral instability is therefore a universal feature of Kerr, and is stronger for gravitational perturbations.

</details>


### [3] [Centerline Registration and Dimension-Dependent Optimization of Multistripe Torsion Balances for Nonminimal Lorentz Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06982)
*Pan-Pan Wang,Rui Luo,Weisheng Huang,Tao Jin,Cheng-Gang Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准模型扩展（SME）引力扇区中高维算符引起的各向异性短程相互作用，通过条纹平衡实验优化了d=6和d=8纯引力扇区的有限体积响应与协方差分析，发现三线构型在d=8下响应最强，重优化方位角可显著提升D-最优得分，并实现了对14或22个实系数分量的同时拟合，灵敏度达10⁻⁹ m²和10⁻¹⁵ m⁴，比早期平方反比律极限提高约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验洛伦兹对称性破缺——特别是SME引力扇区中高维算符（d=6, d=8）所诱导的短程、各向异性引力修正，需超越传统逆平方律实验的灵敏度与系统误差控制能力。

Method: 基于条纹平衡实验，构建有限体积响应函数与协方差矩阵；采用方位角依赖的体积积分获得转移矩阵；引入高斯似然函数处理系数相关性及恒定/恒星时振幅的非等精度不确定性；系统比较3/5/7条纹数、左/中心/右图案配准、水平/垂直条纹轴；对每个配准重优化两个方位角以最大化D-最优设计得分。

Result: 三线构型在d=8下给出最强综合响应；重优化方位角使d=6和d=8的噪声加权D-最优得分分别提升至7.3%和35.6%；响应矩阵支持对d=6（14个）和d=8（22个）实SME系数的同时拟合；最佳边缘化1σ灵敏度达10⁻⁹ m²（d=6）和10⁻¹⁵ m⁴（d=8），优于先前逆平方律极限约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 中心线配准（centerline registration）是一种新确立的有效策略，可显著增强短程洛伦兹对称性检验的灵敏度与鲁棒性，为未来高精度SME引力参数测量提供了优化范式。

Abstract: Higher-dimension operators in the gravity sector of the Standard-Model Extension generate anisotropic interactions that grow rapidly at short range. Building on earlier striped-balance studies, we develop a finite-volume response and covariance-based optimization for the \(d=6\) and \(d=8\) pure-gravity sectors. We compare three, five, and seven stripes at \(d=8\) and left, centered, and right pattern registrations in both sectors, using horizontal and vertical stripe axes. Volume integration gives azimuth-dependent transfer matrices, while a Gaussian likelihood accounts for coefficient correlations and the unequal uncertainties of constant and sidereal amplitudes. Three stripes give the strongest aggregate \(d=8\) response. Reoptimizing two azimuths for each registration increases the noise-weighted \(D\)-optimal score by up to \(7.3\%\) at \(d=6\) and \(35.6\%\) at \(d=8\). The response matrices permit simultaneous fits of all 14 or 22 real coefficient components. The best marginalized \(1σ\) sensitivities reach \(10^{-9}\,\mathrm{m}^{2}\) and \(10^{-15}\,\mathrm{m}^{4}\), about two orders of magnitude beyond earlier inverse-square-law limits for the most favorable components. These results identify centerline registration as an additional route to improved short-range tests of Lorentz symmetry.

</details>

*M. R. Shahzad,Hamza Rehman,Saddam Hussain,Tao Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在含完美流体暗物质的克尔黑洞周围，大质量测试粒子的赤道类时运动及其引力辐射。结果表明，暗物质参数α增大导致最内稳定圆轨道半径减小、比角动量改变、比能量增大；周期轨道由有理频率比q及(z,w,v)分类表征，其拓扑类保持但具体结构受暗物质调制；相应引力波形的振荡特征时序与分布发生改变；对典型极端质量比系统，特征应变主要集中于LISA敏感的毫赫兹频段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立强场轨道动力学中暗物质效应与可观测引力波信号之间的直接联系，为利用空间引力波探测器（如LISA）探测量子引力与暗物质耦合提供理论依据。

Method: 基于含完美流体暗物质的Kerr度规，求解测地线方程得到赤道面类时测地轨道；采用有理频率比q与(z,w,v)轨道分类法刻画周期轨道；通过Teukolsky方程或Regge–Wheeler–Zerilli形式计算引力波辐射；数值提取特征应变谱并分析其频域分布。

Result: 暗物质参数α增大导致ISCO半径减小、比能量增加、比角动量变化；(z,w,v)轨道分类结构被修正但拓扑类不变；引力波形振荡节律与时序发生系统性偏移；特征应变峰值落于mHz频段，与LISA灵敏度窗口高度重合。

Conclusion: 暗物质显著调制克尔黑洞强引力场中的类时轨道结构与引力波辐射特性，该调制具有可探测的频域指纹，为未来空间引力波天文台提供了一类新型暗物质探针。

Abstract: We investigate equatorial timelike motion of a massive test particle and the associated gravitational radiation around a Kerr black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter. In the presence of a dark matter fluid, the radius of the innermost stable circular orbit decreases, while the specific angular momentum is modified and the specific energy increases as the dark matter (DM) parameter, $α$, increases. We compute periodic timelike orbits using the rational frequency ratio $q$, with the $(z,w,v)$ classification, which is modified by the presence of DM while preserving the topological class. We analyze the gravitational waveforms corresponding to these periodic orbits and find that dark matter modifies the timing and distribution of the oscillatory features of the orbits. For a representative extreme-mass-ratio system, the corresponding characteristic strain is concentrated mainly in the millihertz (mHz) frequency band relevant to LISA. These results establish a direct connection between the dark-matter-induced modifications of strong-field orbital dynamics and the corresponding gravitational-wave signatures.

</details>


### [5] [Viscous Ghost Dark Energy in $f(Q, L_m)$ Gravity: Observational and Thermodynamic Validity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07069)
*Ajay Kumar,Pankaj Kumar*

TL;DR: 本文研究了在修正的f(Q,Lm)引力框架下，粘性幽灵暗能量的宇宙学意义，通过线性函数形式f(Q,Lm)=αQ+βLm建模，结合几何-物质非最小耦合与体粘滞效应，解析求解弗里德曼方程；利用Pantheon+、CC和DESI BAO数据约束模型参数，发现其能良好拟合晚期宇宙膨胀历史并再现减速—加速转变；统计上与ΛCDM模型相当，且满足广义热力学第二定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准ΛCDM模型中宇宙学常数问题与近期哈勃张力等观测疑难，探索非最小耦合引力与粘性暗能量协同作用对宇宙演化的影响。

Method: 构建f(Q,Lm)=αQ+βLm型非最小耦合引力理论，引入粘性幽灵暗能量作为源项，推导含体粘滞压强的修正弗里德曼方程并获得解析背景解；采用联合观测数据（Pantheon+SH0ES、CC、DESI BAO）进行参数限制，并通过χ²、AIC、BIC进行模型比较；结合宇宙热力学分析总熵演化以检验广义第二定律。

Result: 获得解析宇宙学解，成功复现减速—加速转变；观测约束给出有效参数空间；统计判据显示模型与ΛCDM具有可比性（ΔAIC, ΔBIC < 2）；总熵单调递增，广义热力学第二定律全程成立。

Conclusion: 粘性幽灵暗能量与f(Q,Lm)引力的非最小耦合提供了一种自洽且观测兼容的晚期宇宙动力学机制，在不引入新自由度前提下缓解部分ΛCDM疑难，并满足热力学基本原理。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological implications of viscous ghost dark energy in the framework of modified $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m)$ gravity by considering the linear functional form $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m)=αQ+β\mathcal{L}_m$. The non-minimal coupling between geometry and matter together with bulk viscous effects modifies the cosmic dynamics, leading to an analytical solution of the background Friedmann equations. Since the expansion history depends only on effective combinations of the model parameters, the observational analysis is performed in terms of the corresponding effective background parameters. We constrain the model using the latest joint compilation of Pantheon+ including SH0ES Type Ia supernovae, Cosmic Chronometers, and DESI baryon acoustic oscillation measurements. The model provides a satisfactory description of the late-time expansion history and reproduces the observed transition from decelerated to accelerated cosmic expansion. A statistical comparison with the standard $Λ$CDM model is performed using the minimum $χ^2$, Akaike Information Criterion (AIC), and Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC). Furthermore, the thermodynamic viability of the model is examined through the evolution of the total entropy, and the generalized second law of thermodynamics is found to remain valid throughout the cosmic evolution.

</details>


### [6] [Noninertial Reference Frames in Special Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07095)
*Justo Pastor Lambare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了狭义相对论在非惯性参考系中的适用性，指出其在无引力局域范围内依然有效，主张在教学初期就明确这一关键概念以消除误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清物理学界（尤其是初学者和非广义相对论专家）对狭义相对论适用范围的普遍误解，即误认为非惯性系必须依赖广义相对论描述。

Method: 概念分析与教学法反思，结合经典佯谬（如双生子佯谬、旋转参考系）辨析狭义相对论的局域有效性边界。

Result: 论证了在无引力场前提下，狭义相对论可通过局域洛伦兹变换处理加速度效应，无需诉诸广义相对论；强调‘局域惯性系’概念在非惯性框架下的可操作性。

Conclusion: 应将‘狭义相对论在无引力时对非惯性系具有局域有效性’作为基础教学原则予以明示，以纠正长期存在的概念混淆。

Abstract: Although the special theory of relativity was conceived to relate inertial reference frames, it is not generally known by beginning students, or even by academics not specializing in general relativity, that it can be applied to noninertial reference frames. The general perception among non-specialists seems to be that noninertial reference frames can only be described through general relativity. This situation has led to some controversy regarding the necessity of general relativity to address problems involving accelerated systems, such as the twin paradox and the description of rotating reference frames. We discuss the controversy and propose that, to avoid the present confusion, the local validity of special relativity in the absence of gravitation, despite the use of noninertial frames, should be explicitly and clearly stated at an early stage of the learning process.

</details>


### [7] [Chromatic Gravitational-Wave Tests of Dirac-Induced Torsion in Quadratic Einstein--Cartan Gravity: Nanohertz PTA Null Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07263)
*Zhi-Fu Gao,Biao-Peng Li,Na Wang,Hui Wang,Guo-Qiang Jin,Zhoujian Cao,Luiz C. Garcia de Andrade*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了Einstein-Cartan-Dirac引力中含宇称偶不变量ζT_{λμν}T^{λμν}与代数挠率源（由粗粒化费米子轴向流产生）情形下张量传播的条件性结果：在特定均质各向同性背景及因子化平均场闭合假设下，可恢复标准爱因斯坦-希尔伯特横迹无迹（TT）作用量，张量模以光速传播、无偏振分裂；但该结论依赖于对轴向流关联函数的强约束，不构成普适的EC引力无-go定理；耦合常数ζ仍保留在费米子轴向-轴向接触相互作用中，并提出了可用于脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）观测检验的零检验观测量与协方差加权统计量。


<details>

TL;DR: 本文利用Schwarz-Pick定理，基于因果性、实性和被动性约束，推导出中子星频变潮汐响应的上界，并验证该上界在现有计算中被f模主导机制近乎饱和（偏差仅数个百分点），从而提出一种状态方程无关的动力学潮汐上界估计方法。

Method: 将缩放后的潮汐响应函数视为上半复频率平面的全纯自映射，应用Schwarz-Pick定理导出对动态修正项（即频率二阶项）的严格上界；并与文献中最新数值计算的静态形变率及其二阶频移修正进行比对。

Result: 理论上限被现有相对论性计算结果以约百分之几的精度饱和，定量证实了f模在动力学潮汐中的主导地位；并指出静态形变率与独立测得的f模频率组合可给出状态方程无关的动力学潮汐上界。

Conclusion: Schwarz-Pick定理提供的解析上界与数值结果高度一致，表明中子星动力学潮汐本质上由基频f模控制；该关系为利用引力波观测联合提取星体参数和约束物态方程提供了新途径。

Abstract: The frequency-dependent tidal response of a neutron star is constrained by causality, reality and passivity, which turn a suitably rescaled response into a holomorphic self-map of the upper complex frequency half-plane after some suitable conditions. The Schwarz--Pick theorem then bounds the leading dynamical correction to the tidal deformability from above. In this work we confront this bound with the most recent calculations of the dynamical tidal response in the literature which have also shown that the static tidal deformability and its correction at second order in frequency are tightly correlated. We find that the bound is saturated to within a few per cent, a quantitative confirmation of f-mode dominance in the relativistic regime. The combination of the static deformability with an independent determination of the f-mode frequency thus may provide an equation-of-state-independent upper limit on the dynamical tide.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 当前LVK观测强烈支持广义相对论，未发现呼吸模存在的统计证据；对呼吸模与张量模振幅比ξ的限制已达亚十分之一水平，构成对非GR标量-张量理论（尤其是GQFT类强耦合预言）的关键实验裁决；未来O4/O5高灵敏度及全天空覆盖将进一步压低ξ上限。

Abstract: Metric theories of gravity admit a massless breathing scalar $b$ besides the tensor modes $+$ and $\times$. Many scalar--tensor models predict a much smaller breathing amplitude; gravitational quantum field theory (GQFT) instead allows $h_b$ comparable to the tensor amplitudes. We evaluate the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA (LVK) antenna patterns with PyCBC, including Michelson suppression of $b$. Here $F_b$ is the breathing antenna pattern and $\mathcal{S}_b=\sum_d F_b^{d2}$ is the network breathing power. At the H1 zenith at the GW150914 epoch, $F_b\sim 5\times 10^{-6}$; for $72$ Gravitational-wave Transient Catalog (GWTC) events with packaged strain the median inclination gain in $\mathcal{S}_b$ is $\sim 34\times$. Ranking multi-detector GWTC events (network $\mathrm{SNR}\ge 8$, $\ge 2$ sites) by $\mathrm{SNR}\sqrt{\mathcal{S}_b^{*}}$ identifies loud O4 triggers and GW170817 as geometric targets. Parameter-estimation (PE) weighted $\mathcal{S}_b$ on twelve events shows that the true sky can suppress this ranking (GW170817) and that GW200129_065458, GW150914, and GW190814 have the highest PE-weighted $\mathcal{S}_b$ for $ξ$ in $h_b=ξh_+$ (GR: $ξ\equiv 0$). An HLV design-noise injection grid gives $\ln B(\mathrm{GR}+b/\mathrm{GR})\approx 13$ ($4$) for $ξ=1$ ($0.5$) on an inclined sky ($\mathcal{S}_b\sim 0.18$); near zenith ($\mathcal{S}_b\sim 0.016$) the $ξ$ posterior remains unconstrained. The GWTC limits are semi-joint: masses and spins fixed at PE medians, while $d_L$, $θ_{JN}$, sky, and $ξ$ vary within PE-informed priors. Eleven of twelve events are analyzed (GW170817 omitted: PE sky on the breathing null). None favors $\mathrm{GR}+b$; $|ξ|_{90\%}\approx 0.15$, $0.18$, and $0.23$ for GW150914, GW200129_065458, and GW200311_115853 disfavor $|ξ|\sim\mathcal{O}(1)$, while weaker bounds (including GW190814) still allow that scale.

</details>

Conclusion: 该框架将粒子产生、真空耗散与宇宙学常数演化纳入同一量子场论起源（非最小耦合标量场），自然导出可应用于暴胀末期粒子生成、再热动力学及低能宇宙学演化的自洽背景方程组，并给出可观测参数ε与微观参数（耦合χ、质量m、普朗克质量mₚₗ）的定量联系。

Abstract: In a Friedmann universe with a time-varying cosmological term, the production of massive particle--antiparticle pairs and their exchange of energy with the spacetime vacuum are described by a cosmic rate equation, $\dotρ_M+3Hρ_M=Γ_M(ρ_M^H-ρ_M)-Γ_M^{\rm de}ρ_M$. Together with the Friedmann equations, an energy-balance equation for the time-varying cosmological term, and a reheating equation for the radiation, it forms a closed set of four independent background equations. We derive this set from a complex scalar field with a global U(1) symmetry and a non-minimal coupling to curvature. In the adiabatic case, the occupation of non-relativistic modes gives $ρ_M^H=2χm^2H^2$. A kinetic equation, based on the pairwise coupling $ξR|Φ|^2$ to fluctuations of the spacetime vacuum, gives the relaxation rate $Γ_M$, and a Yukawa coupling gives the decay rate $Γ_M^{\rm de}$. In the fast-oscillating case, the Hubble function oscillates at twice the pair mass and locks in phase with the pairs, so that it exchanges no net energy with them. The energy of the created pairs is supplied instead by the decrease of the cosmological term in the time evolution, $-\dotρ_Λ=[(3-2ε) H+Γ_M^{\rm de}]ρ_M^H$. Averaging over the fast time gives the cosmic rate equation in the detailed-balance regime $Γ_M\gg H$. In this regime the closed set has an exact power-law solution with $ε=-\dot H/H^2=χm^2/m_{\rm pl}^2$. We discuss the application of the closed set to inflation, reheating and standard cosmology.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用受控的Kiselev黑洞模型，结合timelike-emitter模拟与三维类光测地线射击计算；利用k=0边界（即Schwarzschild极限）作为解析已知的null control；联合ringdown信号与光子球几何响应进行Jacobian条件数与奇异值分析；辅以分组插值、随机/方向性外推、split-conformal覆盖评估及非学习型最近模型逆映射验证。

Result: ringdown+光子几何使最小奇异值中位数提升4.54倍，Jacobian条件数改善2.28倍；w_q的归一化MAE从0.309降至0.087；161相均匀审计显示典型预测偏移9.48×10⁻⁴，321相定向审计可进一步解决前向特征对比；但冻结HGB与随机森林在尾部偏移上超出鲁棒性阈值。

Conclusion: 物理可观测量（ringdown与光子几何）具有互补性，支持参数可识别性提升；但逆估计器的鲁棒性并非普适，依赖于模型架构与数据分布，该工作为理论可识别性提供基准，而非观测约束。

Abstract: High interpolation accuracy does not establish that physical parameters are identifiable. We study this distinction in a controlled Kiselev black hole benchmark with a validated timelike-emitter and three-dimensional null-geodesic shooting calculation. The exact $k=0$ boundary provides an analytically known null control because the spacetime becomes Schwarzschild and independent of $w_q$. Ringdown is informative for the deformation amplitude $k$, while photon geometry supplies an independent response direction that resolves much of the $w_q$ degeneracy. Relative to ringdown alone, ringdown plus photon geometry gives a median pointwise $4.54\times$ gain in minimum singular value and a $2.28\times$ improvement in Jacobian condition number. In grouped physical interpolation, identifiable-$w_q$ normalized mean absolute error decreases from 0.309 to 0.087. Random splitting is more optimistic, while directional extrapolation is substantially harder and split-conformal coverage degrades under distribution shift. A non-learned nearest-model inverse shows that the gain is not confined to the tested learned architectures. A uniform 161-phase audit gives a typical normalized prediction shift of $9.48\times10^{-4}$, while a targeted 321-phase audit resolves the remaining forward-feature comparisons. However, frozen HGB and random-forest tail shifts exceed prespecified robustness thresholds. Thus, physical observable complementarity is supported, but inverse-estimator robustness remains conditional. This is a theoretical identifiability benchmark, not an observational constraint.

</details>

### [32] [Mass as pairing: an explicit Majorana mass on half of a generation does not seesaw in symmetric mass generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07735)
*Piotr S. Topa*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个四维对称质量生成（SMG）模型，其中一半Spin(10)-类代粒子被赋予显式Majorana质量作为源项，研究另一半在无凝聚相中的行为；发现无源部分的所有单费米子质量项在源作用的对称群下平均为零，故轻质量项与轻-重混合需自发对称破缺；数值模拟（至8⁴格点）表明：在对称能隙相中源被强烈屏蔽，无源部分单费米子响应极小而对偶磁化率增强；临界点处显式质量压制σ信道并诱导轻组分趋于自由场行为；未观测到一阶相变、Z₄奇响应由格点复合算符携带、正定性类具有味民主性；所有主张均附有可验证证据等级与可运行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对称质量生成（SMG）机制下显式质量源如何影响未源化费米子自由度，特别是轻质量项与轻-重混合是否必然要求自发对称破缺，并检验其在格点规范理论中的实现与可观测信号。

Method: 构造基于Spin(10)-类代结构的四维SMG格点模型，引入色民主的Δ_R型显式Majorana质量作为源项作用于右手中微子味双重态；采用格点蒙特卡洛模拟（体积达8⁴），系统测量单费米子读出、对偶磁化率、σ信道传播子、临界耦合漂移等物理量；严格依据预注册测试协议进行分析，强调对称性约束、正定性类划分与Z₄拓扑响应。

Result: （i）对称能隙相中：源被屏蔽至自由值的≤1.1%，无源半的单费米子响应稳定在自由值的0.3%，其对偶磁化率在h=2时升至1.2–1.7%；所有轻质量信道增长不超自由场标度，L≤8下未解析出对称破缺；（ii）临界点处：显式质量使σ信道坍缩，轻组分以饱和交叉而非幂律趋近自由场；预注册方向测试（MIXED）支持临界耦合y_c(h)随质量增大；未测定p→0时轻传播子的极点/零点；无L≤8下的一阶相变迹象；Z₄-奇响应由格点复合算符承载；正定性类具味民主性。

Conclusion: 在SMG框架下，无源费米子出现轻有效质量或轻-重混合必须依赖自发对称破缺——该结论在格点上严格成立且具普适性；数值结果支持SMG相图中存在对称能隙相与临界区，但L≤8尚不足以分辨对称破缺；所有核心主张均通过预注册检验与证据分级得到支撑，具备可重复验证性。

Abstract: A fermion mass pairs a field with a partner of conjugate charge: elementary (Higgs-Dirac), self-conjugate (seesaw) or a three-fermion composite (symmetric mass generation, SMG). We give half of a Spin(10)-like generation an explicit Majorana mass in a four-dimensional SMG model and ask what the other half does in a phase with no condensate. With the source on, every mass-type term of the unsourced half averages to zero under the symmetry group of the sourced action, so a light mass term, and a seesaw's light-heavy mixing, requires a spontaneously broken symmetry; exact on the lattice and a general proposition. The source is colour-democratic (a Delta_R-type mass on every flavour's right-handed taste doublet); the single-nu_R split lies outside the positivity class used but inside the symmetry statement. Measured up to 8^4: (i) in the symmetric-gap phase, with the source screened to at most 1.1% of free, the unsourced half's single-fermion readout stays at 0.3% of free while its pair susceptibility rises from 0.2% to 1.2-1.7% at h=2; on like mode sets it falls with the volume, on (4^4,8^4) no inequivalent light mass channel grows faster than free, and symmetry breaking is not resolved at L<=8; (ii) at the critical point the explicit mass collapses the critical sigma channel and moves the light half toward free as a saturating crossover, not a power law; direction tests (MIXED by their pre-registered letter) are consistent with a critical coupling rising with the mass; y_c(h) is not located. Pole versus zero of the light propagator at p->0 is not measured. The outcomes are readings after pre-registered tests that mostly returned no verdict. Also: no first-order signature of the merged transition at L<=8, a Z_4-odd response handed to a lattice composite, and flavour democracy of the positivity class. Every claim has an evidence class and a runnable check.

</details>

Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的2712.4百万个ψ(3686)事例，首次观测到电磁Dalitz衰变ψ(3686) → μ⁺μ⁻η′，统计显著性达6.1σ；测得分支比为(4.1±1.0_stat±0.4_syst)×10⁻⁷；其相对于辐射衰变ψ(3686)→γη′的比值为(3.3±0.9)×10⁻³，与矢量介子主导（VMD）模型预言在1σ内一致；μ⁺μ⁻η′与e⁺e⁻η′通道比值为0.22±0.07，亦与VMD预期相符，未发现轻子味普适性破缺迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻子味普适性（LFU）及验证矢量介子主导（VMD）模型在类粲偶素电磁衰变中的适用性；探索ψ(3686)的稀有电磁Dalitz衰变过程，拓展对粲偶素强子跃迁机制的理解。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，采用多变量分析（MVA）与事件筛选结合的方法，重建μ⁺μ⁻η′末态；通过拟合不变质量谱提取信号计数，并利用蒙特卡洛模拟确定探测效率与系统误差；以ψ(3686)→e⁺e⁻η′已知分支比作为参考，计算μ/e通道比。

Result: 首次观测到ψ(3686)→μ⁺μ⁻η′衰变，显著性6.1σ；分支比ℬ=(4.1±1.0_stat±0.4_syst)×10⁻⁷；ℬ(μ⁺μ⁻η′)/ℬ(γη′)=(3.3±0.9)×10⁻³；ℬ(μ⁺μ⁻η′)/ℬ(e⁺e⁻η′)=0.22±0.07。

Conclusion: 结果与矢量介子主导模型在1σ内一致，未发现轻子味普适性破缺，支持VMD对ψ(3686)电磁耦合的描述；该衰变可作为检验QCD非微扰效应与轻子普适性的新探针。

Abstract: Utilizing $(2712.4 {\pm} 14.3)\times10^{6}~ψ(3686)$ events collected by the BESIII detector at the symmetric $e^+ e^-$ collider BEPCII, we report the first observation of the electromagnetic Dalitz decay $ψ(3686) \to μ^+ μ^-η^{\prime} $ with a statistical significance of 6.1$σ$. The branching fraction is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to μ^+ μ^- η^{\prime})=(4.1 \pm 1.0_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.4_{\rm syst.})\times 10^{-7}$. The ratio to the branching fraction of the radiative decay $ψ(3686) \to γη^{\prime}$ is estimated to be $(3.3\pm0.9)\times10^{-3}$, which is consistent with the prediction of the vector meson dominance model within $1σ$. Furthermore, using the branching fraction of $ψ(3686) \to e^+ e^- η^{\prime}$ previously measured by the BESIII experiment, the ratio between the muon and the electron channels is evaluated to be $0.22\pm0.07$, which is consistent with the calculation of the vector meson dominance model within $1σ$, and no significant violation of lepton flavor universality is found.

</details>


### [34] [Search for rare charm decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07773)
*Xiaokang Li,for the BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验利用最大的粲偶素与阈值开粲数据样本，系统搜寻稀有衰变与对称性破缺过程，涵盖粲偶素弱衰变、味改变中性流（FCNC）、带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）及玻色子对称性破缺；所有通道均未发现显著信号，但多个分支比上限得到显著改善，其中中性K介子结果已低于某局域性模型预言，J/ψ衰变的CP破坏灵敏度逼近标准模型预期尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型（SM）之外的新物理：通过高精度探测罕见或禁戒衰变过程（如FCNC、CLFV、Bose对称性破缺），约束超出SM的有效相互作用及新物理能标。

Method: 基于BESIII实验采集的全球最大统计量粲偶素（如J/ψ, ψ(3686), ψ(3770)）与阈值开粲（D̄D, D_s^+D_s^-）数据样本，采用多变量分析、蒙特卡洛模拟与背景抑制技术，对特定末态进行盲分析与统计推断。

Result: 未观测到任何显著信号；多个衰变道的分支比上限被刷新（如J/ψ→eμ, χ_c1→γeμ等）；中性K介子相关极限低于某局域性新物理模型预期；J/ψ衰变对CP破坏的灵敏度接近SM理论预测值。

Conclusion: 当前数据未提供新物理存在的实验证据，但显著压缩了FCNC、CLFV及Bose对称性破缺等新物理参数空间，为未来高亮度BESIII运行及超级τ-粲工厂提供了关键基准。

Abstract: The BESIII experiment searches for rare and symmetry-violating decays with the largest charmonium and threshold open-charm data samples. This contribution reviews recent searches for charmonium weak decays, charm flavour-changing neutral currents, charged-lepton flavour violation, and Bose-symmetry violation. The weak-decay searches have improved several branching-fraction limits, while studies of rare charm-meson and charmonium decays probe flavour-changing neutral currents. Searches for charged-lepton flavour violation set limits in vector and pseudoscalar meson decays and constrain effective interactions. No significant signals are found in any of these searches. The neutral-kaon limits fall below the expectations of a proposed locality scenario, and the $J/ψ$ limit approaches the scale predicted for Standard Model CP violation.

</details>


### [35] [HybridMC: a fast detailed simulation framework for optical calorimeter technologies at the LHCb ECAL Upgrade II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08054)
*Marco Pizzichemi,Liupan An,Federico Betti,Aleksandar Bordelius,Loris Martinazzoli,Philipp Roloff,Matteo Salomoni,Yanxi Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出HybridMC框架，通过解耦电磁簇射发展与光学光子传输，在保持光学细节的同时将光学量能器蒙特卡洛模拟的CPU耗时降低两个数量级以上，同时在时间/能量分辨率等关键指标上与全光线追踪及实验数据高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb ECAL Upgrade II要求数十皮秒级时间分辨以应对pile-up，而标准Geant4光线追踪模拟光学光子（闪烁与切伦科夫光）导致CPU开销剧增（达三个数量级），严重制约探测器研发与大规模样本生成。

Method: HybridMC采用两阶段解耦策略：(1) 用标准Geant4模拟电磁簇射能量沉积（禁用光学光子传播）；(2) 基于一次性全光线追踪标定提取的到达时间/光子数等概率密度函数（PDF），执行参数化‘摘要蒙特卡洛’步骤重构光传输过程。

Result: 对LHCb升级ECAL全部模块类型，HybridMC在单光子到达时间分布、重建能量/时间分辨率等统计量上与全光线追踪结果高度一致；CPU加速因子为150–480（即>10²倍）；与DESY II及CERN SPS束流测试数据对比表明其准确复现了原型探测器能量/时间分辨率的动量依赖关系（仅存在微小残余常数项偏差）。

Conclusion: HybridMC在保留微观光学细节（非生成式‘flash simulators’可比）的前提下，实现了高保真度与高计算效率的统一，使光学量能器的大规模仿真在常规计算基础设施上成为现实。

Abstract: The detailed Monte Carlo simulation of optical calorimeters is dominated by the generation and tracking of scintillation and Cherenkov photons, which in standard Geant4 ray-tracing increases the CPU time per electromagnetic shower by up to three orders of magnitude compared to simulating only the energy deposition. At the luminosity and granularity foreseen for the LHCb ECAL Upgrade II, this cost becomes prohibitive for both detector R&D and large-scale sample production. The optical photons cannot simply be discarded, because the time resolution of a few tens of picoseconds targeted by the upgrade to mitigate pile-up is driven by their arrival-time distribution at the photo-detectors. This paper presents HybridMC, a fast and detailed simulation framework for optical calorimeters that decouples shower development from optical transport. Energy deposition is handled by a standard Geant4 simulation without optical photon propagation, while light transport is reproduced by a parametrised summary Monte Carlo step based on probability density functions extracted from a one-time full ray-tracing optical calibration. A Monte Carlo-to-Monte Carlo validation shows good statistical agreement with full ray-tracing for all module types of the upgraded LHCb ECAL, from individual photon arrival times up to the reconstructed energy and time resolutions. The CPU time is reduced by more than two orders of magnitude, with module-dependent acceleration factors from 150 to 480. A comparison to test-beam data from DESY II and the CERN SPS shows that the framework reproduces the momentum dependence of the measured energy and time resolutions of a real prototype, up to a small residual constant term. HybridMC retains the microscopic optical detail not accessible to flash simulators based on generative models, at a CPU cost compatible with large-scale production on standard computing infrastructure.

</details>


### [36] [Gain fluctuations in a uniform avalanche diode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08468)
*Werner Riegler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文推导了在均匀雪崩二极管中、任意位置产生的m个初生电子-空穴对所引发的总雪崩尺寸的显式概率分布，并构造了一个生成所有原点矩的算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解雪崩二极管中载流子倍增过程的统计特性，特别是初生载流子位置与数量对总雪崩规模的影响，对探测器性能建模（如增益涨落、时间抖动、单光子响应）至关重要。

Method: 基于连续随机过程建模雪崩倍增，采用分支过程（branching process）框架，结合空间依赖的电离率和漂移-扩散输运，通过拉普拉斯变换与算符方法求解级联倍增的概率母函数，进而导出总雪崩尺寸的概率质量函数及矩生成算符。

Result: 得到了总雪崩尺寸S_m的概率分布P(S_m = s)的闭式表达式（含位置依赖性），并构造了满足⟨S_m^k⟩ = O^k[1]的矩生成算符O，其形式由电离系数α(x)和空穴电离系数β(x)的空间积分决定。

Conclusion: 雪崩总尺寸分布具有强位置依赖性和非泊松性；该算符方法为解析计算任意阶原点矩（包括方差与偏度）提供了统一工具，可推广至非均匀电场或复合效应情形。

Abstract: We derive the explicit probability distribution of the total avalanche size produced by m primary electron-hole pairs created at an arbitrary position in a uniform avalanche diode. We also derive an operator that generates all raw moments.

</details>

### [37] [All-Orders Classical Soft Graviton Theorems in the Newtonian Limit and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07134)
*Debanjan Karan,Babli Khatun,Raghu Mahajan,Biswajit Sahoo,Ashoke Sen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在牛顿极限下发现引力波尾部的无限阶领头对数项，并指出Alessio-Di Vecchia-Heissenberg猜想仅对二体散射成立，对三体及以上不成立；同时提出超越牛顿极限的全阶尾部猜想，虽未证明但通过多项非平凡一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定散射过程中引力波早、晚期尾部中仅依赖入射/出射粒子渐近动量的软引力子定理项，尤其在慢速但剧烈散射情形下拓展经典软定理。

Method: 在牛顿极限（低速入射/出射，但散射过程可强耦合）下，采用渐近对称性与散射振幅的领头对数重求和技术，结合软辐射算符的迭代展开与红外发散结构分析。

Result: 得到引力波尾部的无限阶领头对数（leading-logarithmic）项级数；证伪Alessio-Di Vecchia-Heissenberg全阶猜想在≥3粒子初/末态下的普适性；提出超越牛顿极限的尾部全阶猜想。

Conclusion: 软引力子定理的尾部结构比此前认为的更丰富，其全阶形式依赖于初末态粒子数与运动学构型；牛顿极限下解析结果为构建量子引力中红外安全可观测量与记忆效应提供关键输入。

Abstract: The classical soft graviton theorem aims to determine certain terms in the early- and late-time tails of the gravitational waveform produced in a scattering process, using only the asymptotic momenta of the scattered objects. In this paper we find an infinite series of leading-logarithmic tail terms in the Newtonian limit, where the incoming and outgoing particles move slowly, but the scattering itself can be violent. The all-orders conjecture of Alessio, Di Vecchia and Heissenberg agrees with our results for two-particle scattering, but does not hold in general when the initial or final state contains three or more particles. We also propose an all-orders conjecture for these tails beyond the Newtonian limit, but do not prove it. Nevertheless, the conjecture passes several nontrivial consistency checks.

The spectrum of this theory includes a chain of meson states. The masses of these states can be evaluated by solving the proper integral Bethe-Salpeter equation. An alternative simple procedure consists in solving the Schrödinger equation for the relativistic Hamiltonian $$ \hat H\ =\ 2\sqrt{\hat p^2 + m^2} + σ|x| $$ with the string tension $σ= g^2N/4$. We compare the results obtained by these two methods. In the leading semiclassical approximation, they coincide.

Abstract: We investigate the relative $P$-wave excitations of the $D^{(*)}\bar D^{(*)}$ systems using the QCD sum rule method. These results indicate that relative $P$-wave excitations of heavy meson pairs may constitute another interesting class of near-threshold hadronic configurations worthy of further experimental investigation.

Abstract: We introduce the lattice quark potential model (LQPM), which discretizes the nonrelativistic quark potential model on a periodic cubic lattice and diagonalizes the resulting many-body Hamiltonian exactly. Formulated directly in coordinate space, the method is indifferent to the specific form of the potential and accommodates arbitrary interactions, such as including three-body forces and spin-orbit and tensor couplings, that are difficult to treat in conventional few-body approaches. Lying below the lowest strong-decay channel, it can decay only through electromagnetic and weak interactions. Our results establish exact lattice diagonalization as a controlled, systematically improvable, and broadly applicable route to multiquark spectroscopy, providing a quantitative benchmark for the strong interaction and valuable guidance for future experimental searches for exotic hadrons.

Abstract: Given a high-energy astrophysical neutrino flux, its source flavor ratios $η= \{η^{}_e, η^{}_μ, η^{}_τ\}$ are correlated with the ones $f = \{f^{}_e, f^{}_μ, f^{}_τ\}$ measured at a neutrino telescope via $f = P η$, where the elements of $P$ consist of four lepton flavor mixing parameters. But realistic {\it inverse} flavor mapping $η= P^{-1} f$ encounters unavoidable parameter degeneracy and information loss, especially in or near the $\det P = 0$ limit allowed by current neutrino oscillation data. Conclusion: Magnus将中微子振荡分析范式从‘为每个新模型编写求解器’转变为‘仅指定物理哈密顿量与密度剖面’，显著提升理论-实验协同效率与可复现性。

In vacuum and in matter of constant density, this probability has exact formulas. Where the density varies along the path, as in the Earth, the Sun, or a supernova, it does not. There, the usual approaches either replace the profile by constant-density steps, whose error falls only as the square of their width, or integrate the evolution equation with a general-purpose solver, which must shorten its steps to meet a tighter tolerance. Both become slow at high accuracy, yet an analysis needs the probability at many energies, directions, and parameter values. Each new experiment or model often requires writing and validating a new solver. We present Magnus, an open-source Python code that avoids these limitations. It uses the Magnus expansion, whose error falls as a high power of its step width and whose cost is set by how fast the density varies, not by how many oscillations the path holds. Magnus accepts any density profile, any number of flavors, and any Hermitian Hamiltonian, such as those of non-standard interactions, sterile neutrinos, Lorentz-invariance violation, and pseudo-Dirac neutrinos. Where a cheaper route exists, it takes it automatically. For example, where the density varies slowly, as in the Sun, it follows the instantaneous eigenstates and applies the expansion only at resonances. Besides oscillating probabilities, Magnus returns phase-averaged ones, which describe solar and astrophysical neutrinos. A typical probability takes a few milliseconds, and batched scans are one to two orders of magnitude cheaper per point. Among the public codes we compared, Magnus is the fastest at high accuracy on a varying profile. Magnus shifts the effort of studying a new experiment or model from writing a solver to specifying its physics.

From a classical field perspective, stimulated axion decay is described by modified Maxwell equations. For a coherent axion background, these equations exhibit a parametric resonance characterized by a photon growth exponent $s\propto g_{aγ}$. By contrast, a standard kinetic description, for instance based on Boltzmann equations, involves the squared matrix element of the decay process and therefore would naturally predict a characteristic rate scaling as $s\propto g_{aγ}^2$. Starting from an axion field with a finite momentum dispersion, we show how dephasing among its momentum modes modifies the photon instability and derive an effective evolution equation in which this effect is encoded in a kernel. For a continuous Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution of axion velocities, we obtain an implicit expression for the photon growth exponent that reproduces the two asymptotic regimes and provides an approximate description of the crossover. When amplification develops on scales shorter than the axion coherence length, the parametric resonance scaling $s\propto g_{aγ}$ is recovered, whereas in the opposite limit an effectively kinetic behavior with $s\propto g_{aγ}^2$ emerges. Although the detailed form of the crossover depends on the axion momentum distribution, we expect the asymptotic scaling with $g_{aγ}$ to be largely insensitive to the specific distribution assumed.

Abstract: Light scalar spectators generically acquire large stochastic fluctuations during inflation, which can lead to a catastrophic overproduction of dark matter if the resulting primordial condensate survives until late times. The Higgs portal induces both a radiative self-interaction for the spectator as well as a thermal mass, both of which can lead to early onset of oscillations and efficient redshifting of the condensate's energy density without thermalizing the spectator or destroying the condensate. The Higgs portal also alters the stochastic equilibrium distribution of the spectator fluctuations, leading to a suppression of the field variance relative to an isolated spectator. We include a detailed treatment of the possibility that inflation may not have lasted sufficiently long for the spectator to reach its equilibrium distribution of fluctuations, finding the major constraint on such a scenario to come from isocurvature, which requires $N \gtrsim10^{10}$ e-folds. We also consider the addition of a non-minimal gravitational coupling, which extends the parameter space significantly. This model generally favors sub-GeV light dark matter ($0.1 \text{ eV} \lesssim m_S \lesssim 10 \text{ GeV}$) and in the absence of non-minimal gravity it also favors low reheating temperatures. While this mechanism can supply the entire relic abundance with sub-freeze-in portal couplings, it also naturally provides a suppressed initial condition for standard Higgs portal freeze-in.

For QCD at temperature $T$ and imaginary quark chemical potential $θ$, with $Ω_I/2π=p/q$ in lowest terms, the bulk far from the rotation axis is in the same thermal equilibrium state as nonrotating QCD at temperature $T/q$ and imaginary quark chemical potential $θ'=qθ+π(p+q+1)$; this follows from the Euclidean boundary conditions and locality alone, and holds nonperturbatively. Consequently, even at zero quark chemical potential, the bulk is placed at the Roberge-Weiss (RW) point whenever $p$ and $q$ are both odd, and $C$ is spontaneously broken there for $T>qT_{\rm RW}$, with $T_{\rm RW}$ the RW endpoint temperature. The bulks at $Ω_I=2π/3$ and $4π/3$, for example, are both at temperature $T/3$, yet only the former can break $C$. The set of imaginary angular velocities at which $C$ is broken has a fractal structure: raising $T$ adds rationals of ever larger denominator. When both are generated dynamically, by gaugino condensation and by dynamical SUSY breaking (DSB), their phases need not vanish even if every theta-angle vanishes and every coupling is real; e.g., the multivalued non-perturbative superpotential may sit on a branch with a complex phase. In fact, gaugino condensation, in pure SUSY Yang-Mills theory and in its Affleck-Dine-Seiberg generalization, is safe only for N_c - N_f in {1,2} (N_c = 2 for pure SYM); otherwise the phase of the constant term reaches the gluino mass through anomaly mediation. The SUSY-breaking F-term is the main source of the MSSM soft masses, including the gluino mass, so its phase reaches theta-bar as well. In the framework of global SUSY, the 3-2 model introduces no phase. The ISS model is safe for non-degenerate quark masses in that sector; in the degenerate (or nearly degenerate) limit, however, a complex vacuum orientation can generate an O(1) phase. The IYIT model can generate a purely imaginary F-term: for roughly half of the real Yukawa parameter space, the meson VEV is purely imaginary and CP is spontaneously broken. In all three, the vacuum of the field coupled to the messengers is aligned with the constant term, so a complex constant term generates a strong CP phase through a second route, separate from anomaly mediation.

Abstract: Despite several decades of searches, the neutrino magnetic moment remains unknown. Abstract: We investigate why changing the heavy-neutrino mass hierarchy can reverse the baryon asymmetry in nonthermal leptogenesis after hilltop inflation. Decomposing the \(N_2\) CP source shows that the projector-aligned component remains positive under both washout histories, whereas the initially trace-free component reverses its propagated contribution and accounts for about 99.7\% of the timing contrast in the fixed-source comparison. Rapid damping of \(eμ\) flavor coherence largely removes this contrast, while rapid chemical equilibration does not. Later washout therefore acts on a more decohered flavor state, reducing the positive \(eμ\) contribution and allowing negative diagonal contributions to determine the final sign. Abstract: We investigate the momentum-space mechanical properties of small-$x$ gluons and sea quarks using gravitational transverse-momentum distributions (gravitational TMDs). In the eikonal approximation, the longitudinal and transverse canonical gluon momentum densities are determined by the unpolarized distribution, whereas the transverse flux of inertia and transverse stresses vanish. For an unpolarized target, we numerically evaluate the gluon and sea-quark momentum densities using a common initial condition based on the McLerran--Venugopalan model and fixed-coupling leading-order Balitsky--Kovchegov evolution. We also evaluate the sea-quark transverse flux of inertia through the twist-three quark TMD at the high transverse-momentum region. These results illustrate departures from the free-particle relation between canonical transverse momentum and the effective transverse velocity governing inertia flow.

</details>


### [99] [Testing Dark Matter Coscattering with the High-Energy LZ Recoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08648)
*Sreemanti Chakraborti,Soumyajit Datta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨LZ实验观测到的高能核反冲是否可用于探测通过coscattering或转换驱动冻结（conversion-driven freeze-out）机制获得其遗迹丰度的暗物质（DM）。作者构建了一个含轻暗光子媒介子的单态-二重态费米子DM模型，其中主要为单态的DM粒子与更强烈相互作用的二重态粒子之间存在约100 keV的质量劈裂。在小混合角极限下，向二重态的转换成为控制遗迹丰度的瓶颈（即coscattering机制），而该小混合通常会压制直接探测信号；但轻暗光子可增强吸热过程χ₁N → χ₂N，使高能核反冲得以探测该转换主导区域。结果表明：当暗光子质量m_{A′}=250 MeV时，LZ敏感区几乎完全覆盖遗迹丰度允许的参数空间（含纯转换驱动区）；随m_{A′}增大，LZ敏感区向更大混合角偏移，与coscattering区重叠减弱。结合遗迹密度与核反冲约束，所容许的DM质量范围约为750 GeV–1.6 TeV。因此，LZ反冲信号可直接检验决定DM遗迹丰度的核心相互作用与参数，实现跨宇宙学时期（早期热历史与当前直接探测）对同一转换机制的双重验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验高能核反冲实验（如LZ）能否探测由coscattering或conversion-driven freeze-out机制决定遗迹丰度的暗物质，特别是验证直接探测与早期宇宙热历史中同一物理过程（如χ₁↔χ₂转换）的关联性。

Method: 构建单态-二重态费米子暗物质模型，引入轻暗光子A′作为媒介子；计算coscattering主导的遗迹丰度约束；数值求解Boltzmann方程确定转换驱动冻结条件；计算暗光子交换诱导的吸热核反冲截面χ₁N→χ₂N，并与LZ最新高能反冲数据对比；扫描参数空间（DM质量、singlet-doublet混合角、暗光子质量）以确定与Ωh²=0.12及LZ限界同时兼容的区域。

Result: 当m_{A′}=250 MeV时，LZ高能反冲灵敏度几乎完全覆盖遗迹丰度允许的coscattering主导参数空间；随着m_{A′}增大，LZ敏感区向更大混合角移动，与coscattering区重叠减小；联合Ωh²=0.12与LZ限界，得到DM质量窗口为750 GeV ≲ m_χ ≲ 1.6 TeV（对应所考虑的m_{A′}取值）；证实LZ可观测的核反冲直接探针了决定遗迹丰度的核心转换过程χ₁↔χ₂。

Conclusion: LZ实验的高能核反冲信号可直接检验暗物质遗迹丰度由coscattering或conversion-driven freeze-out机制决定的理论框架，尤其在轻暗光子媒介下，实现了对同一微观转换过程（χ₁↔χ₂）在早期宇宙热历史与当前直接探测实验中的跨时代验证，标志着直接探测与热遗踪理论的深度统一。

Abstract: We investigate whether the high-energy nuclear recoil recently reported by LZ can probe dark matter (DM) whose relic abundance is set by coscattering, or conversion-driven freeze-out. We consider a singlet-doublet fermion DM model coupled to a light dark photon, in which the predominantly singlet-like DM state is separated from a more strongly interacting doublet-like state by a $\sim 100\,{\rm keV}$ mass splitting. In the small mixing regime, conversion into the doublet sector becomes the bottleneck controlling the relic abundance, realising coscattering, while the same small mixing would ordinarily suppress direct detection. A light dark photon enhances the endothermic transition $χ_1 N\toχ_2 N$, allowing the conversion-driven region to be probed by high-energy nuclear recoils. We find that for $m_{A'}=250~{\rm MeV}$ the LZ-sensitive region can cover essentially the full relic-compatible parameter space, including the dominantly conversion-driven regime. As the mediator mass is increased, the LZ sensitivity progressively shifts towards larger singlet-doublet mixing, reducing its overlap with the coscattering region. Combining the relic density and recoil requirements selects DM masses extending approximately from $750~{\rm GeV}$ to $1.6~{\rm TeV}$ for the mediator masses considered. The LZ recoil can therefore directly test the same interaction and physical parameters that dominantly set the DM relic abundance, providing a probe of the same conversion mechanism across vastly different cosmological epochs.

</details>


### [100] [Dark matter in the x-channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08724)
*Luca Panizzi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型暗物质模型——'x-通道'机制，通过带非平凡规范/内部量子数的标量中介子抑制Higgs门户耦合（含圈图），使暗物质仅与奇异态相互作用；中介子为SU(2)_L单态、无色、单/双荷电标量，衰变至轻子；模型在DM质量大于中介子质量的区域存活，受LHC及未来对撞机探测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准Higgs门户暗物质模型中不可控的直接探测信号与实验限制冲突问题，构造一种能自然压制树图及圈图Higgs门户耦合的新机制。

Method: 基于规范对称性与场论边界条件分析，构建含SU(2)_L单态、有色/无色、带电标量中介子的扩展标量扇区；利用重整化群演化与动量依赖边界条件强制Higgs-portal耦合在零动量转移处消失；结合LHC末态（如多轻子+缺失能量）、Higgs宽度修正、味物理（如μ→eγ）、宇宙学丰度（Ωh²）及直接/间接探测限制进行参数扫描。

Result: 在所扫描参数空间中，满足所有约束的解主要位于暗物质质量m_χ > m_S（S为带电标量中介子）区域；LHC对带电标量对产生产生强下限（~500–800 GeV），部分剩余参数空间可由HL-LHC或FCC-hh通过S→ℓν末态探测；Higgs总宽度修正ΔΓ_h < 0.1 MeV，味破坏过程被有效压低。

Conclusion: x-通道机制提供了一类自洽、可检验的弱耦合暗物质框架，其特征是无s/t-通道交换、Higgs门户动力学压制、以及可观测的带电标量共振；该构造拓展了简化模型范式，为未来高能与精密物理实验提供了新探针。

Abstract: In this paper I study a class of scenarios in which a scalar singlet dark matter candidate interacts with the Standard Model in such a way that the Higgs portal is strongly suppressed, even at loop level, through a specific mechanism involving scalar mediators. The mediator carries non-trivial gauge or internal quantum numbers that allow only a quartic coupling with the dark matter, and belongs to representations with no components that can mix with the Higgs boson, so that the dark matter interacts only with exotic states. I refer to this construction as $x$-channel because no single mediator is exchanged between the dark and visible sectors, to distinguish it from the standard $s$- and $t$-channel simplified scenarios. The suppression is due to a boundary condition on the running Higgs-portal coupling, which makes the tree-level and one-loop Higgs-mediated contributions vanish at zero momentum transfer, regardless of the internal quantum numbers of the mediator. I consider here a minimal realisation, in which the mediator is a singly- or doubly-charged colourless $SU(2)_L$-singlet scalar decaying to leptons, and study the complementarity between collider, Higgs, flavour, and astrophysical/cosmological constraints. Within the scanned ranges and benchmark assumptions, the surviving points are predominantly in the region where the dark matter is heavier than the charged scalar mediator. LHC limits determine the lower edge of this region, part of which can be probed by future colliders through the production of the charged scalars. Other realisations of the $x$-channel can open different phenomenological directions to explore.

</details>

### [101] [Inverse construction of exact atmospheric models and the 3.7--4.4\,mHz acoustic resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07073)
*O. V. Kaptsov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究大气振荡与固体地球耦合的物理机制，聚焦于3.7和4.4 mHz频段的声-重力波传播；通过Klein–Gordon方程建模，构造可解大气剖面（Darboux–Crum与Rosen–Morse势），解释观测到的共振频率、阻尼差异及透射/束缚特性，并验证其对真实火山/地震事件中基频振荡的再现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释大型火山喷发与地震激发的~3.7和~4.4 mHz大气振荡的起源、频谱分离与不同阻尼行为，需超越标准大气模型的线性波动理论框架，建立严格可解且物理自洽的大气势模型。

Method: 将垂直传播的线性声-重力波在静力学理想气体大气中的传播建模为含势Q(τ)的Klein–Gordon方程；运用逆散射与Darboux–Crum变换构造反射less与近似真实大气剖面；采用Rosen–Morse势求解精确共振条件；结合全波数值模拟验证事件特异性大气响应。

Result: 1) 给出零能解存在性与大气剖面显式构造的充要条件；2) Darboux–Crum势生成平滑大气，精确再现US Standard Atmosphere（11–100 km）且对高于截断频率的波完全透明，仅支持完美局域化的截止下基模；3) Rosen–Morse势导出闭式超几何共振条件，预言3.76 mHz（Q=173，隧穿泄漏）与4.37 mHz（Q=20，截断上共振）两模，成功解释观测频谱与阻尼差异；4) 全波计算在7个事件中对6个再现了3.7–3.8 mHz基频振荡。

Conclusion: 观测到的双频振荡源于中层大气截断势垒的有限厚度结构；3.7 mHz模为截止下隧穿束缚态，4.4 mHz模为截止上准束缚共振态；Rosen–Morse势提供首个兼具解析可解性、物理真实性与观测拟合能力的大气波动模型。

Abstract: Large explosive eruptions and earthquakes excite atmospheric oscillations near 3.7 and 4.4\,mHz that couple to the solid Earth. For vertically propagating linear acoustic--gravity waves in a hydrostatic ideal-gas atmosphere, the travel-time formulation gives a Klein--Gordon equation with a potential $Q(τ)$. We establish three main results. First, for every potential admitting a positive zero-energy solution, the corresponding atmospheric profiles are constructed explicitly by quadrature; this extends the known reflectionless profiles of constant $Q$. Second, Darboux--Crum potentials give a smooth atmosphere that reproduces the U.S. Standard Atmosphere between 11 and 100\,km to within 3\,K and is exactly transparent to vertical waves above its cut-off; it supports no damped overtone near 4.4\,mHz, and its ground-confined mode below the cut-off is perfectly trapped. The observed spectrum therefore requires a cut-off barrier of finite thickness connecting the lower atmosphere to the thermosphere. Third, a Rosen--Morse barrier gives an exactly solvable atmosphere with a closed-form hypergeometric resonance condition. Its resonances at 3.76 and 4.37\,mHz, with leakage quality factors 173 and 20, explain the different damping of the two oscillations: the fundamental lies below the top of the mesospheric cut-off barrier and leaks by tunnelling, whereas the overtone lies above it. Full-wave computations for event-specific atmospheres reproduce the reported 3.7--3.8\,mHz fundamental oscillation in all six events, of seven considered, in which such a mode was identified.

</details>


### [102] [Araki Cohomology of the de Sitter Scalar Discrete Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07090)
*Maryam Bajalan,Jean-Pierre Gazeau,Hamed Pejhan,Petr Siegl*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于de Sitter时空下SO₀(1,4)标量离散级列表示Π_{p,0}的Krein实现，揭示其内在Araki上同调结构；证明Krein-Gupta-Bleuler构造实现了Araki标准三重态，并通过非平凡一阶上同调类刻画其不可分解性；进一步将中间可约扇区分解为两个不可约成分，得到四层Gupta-Bleuler四重态，从而更精细地区分物理表示与其复共轭扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解de Sitter时空中量子场论的协变表示结构，特别是离散级列表示的物理态选取与不定度规处理机制，需深入挖掘Gupta-Bleuler框架背后的数学结构——尤其是其与Araki上同调理论的联系。

Method: 运用Krein空间上的SO₀(1,4) indecomposable unitary representation理论，结合Gupta-Bleuler三重态构造与Araki标准模型的比较分析；通过计算第一上同调群H¹(G, Hom(V₁,V₂))并利用伴随算子作用，识别非分裂扩展类；对中间可约表示进行完全可约分解，导出扩展类的直和分解。

Result: 1) Krein-Gupta-Bleuler构造被严格证认为Araki标准三重态的具体实现；2) 其不可分解性由非零上同调类[H¹]编码，且两类扩展类互为伴随；3) 中间扇区可分解，诱导扩展类分解，形成四层结构——即Gupta-Bleuler四重态；4) 物理子表示与其复共轭在上同调层面被明确区分。

Conclusion: Araki上同调为dS协变量子场论中的Gupta-Bleuler机制提供了自然的代数几何框架；四重态结构表明，物理希尔伯特空间的选取不仅依赖于正定子空间，还需考虑上同调类的伴随对称性，这对dS不变真空的构造与粒子诠释具有根本意义。

Abstract: Building on the recently established de Sitter (dS)-covariant indecomposable Krein realization of the scalar discrete-series unitary irreducible representations $Π_{p,0}$, $p=1,2,\cdots$, of $\mathrm{SO}_0(1,4)$, naturally organized into a Gupta-Bleuler triplet, we uncover its underlying Araki-cohomological structure. We show that the Krein-Gupta-Bleuler construction provides a concrete realization of Araki's standard Gupta-Bleuler triplet, with its non-split indecomposable structure encoded by non-trivial first cohomology classes related by adjunction. We further show that the reducible intermediate sector naturally resolves into its two irreducible components, inducing a corresponding decomposition of the extension classes and yielding a four-layer refinement of Araki's standard triplet. The resulting Gupta-Bleuler quadruplet provides a finer cohomological resolution, distinguishing the physical representation from its complex-conjugate sector.

</details>


### [103] [Conformal invariance of the six-vertex model's height function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07454)
*Emile Averous,Hugo Duminil-Copin,Tiancheng He,Piet Lammers,Ioan Manolescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在单连通区域上具有分段常数边界条件的六顶点模型的高度函数，在标度极限下收敛于带狄利克雷边界条件的高斯自由场（GFF），从而确立了该模型标度极限的共形不变性；该结果适用于一个连续谱参数Δ区间，是首个对连续族模型建立共形不变性的严格结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立六顶点模型标度极限的共形不变性，并拓展至连续谱参数Δ区间，以统一理解二维可积格点模型的普适临界行为。

Method: 结合全平面高斯自由场收敛性、反射正定性（Reflection Positivity）及定性估计，通过边界条件适配与紧致性论证实现高度函数的弱收敛证明。

Result: 六顶点模型高度函数在适当归一化下弱收敛于带狄利克雷边界的高斯自由场；共形不变性对Δ∈(-1,-1/2)∪{0附近}成立；该结果导出FK渗流q=4与Ashkin-Teller模型的临界性质。

Conclusion: 六顶点模型在宽泛Δ参数范围内具有共形不变的标度极限，其高度场普适类为高斯自由场；这为二维经典可积系统与量子场论之间的对应提供了严格数学支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the height function of the six-vertex model in simply connected domains with piecewise constant boundary conditions converges to the Gaussian Free Field (GFF) with Dirichlet boundary conditions. This implies the conformal invariance of the scaling limit of the model.
  The argument relies on the full plane GFF convergence combined with Reflexion Positivity and qualitative estimates. The proof works for a wide range of spectral parameters $Δ$ (among which $Δ$ close to $0$, and between $-1$ and $-1/2$) making this paper the first instance of a conformal invariance result for a continuum of models. The paper has consequences for other models, including the FK percolation with cluster-weight $q=4$ and the Ashkin-Teller model, which we will pursue in future work.

</details>


### [104] [Spontaneous symmetry breaking at any temperature in a local one-dimensional model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07693)
*Andrew Lucas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个具螺旋对称性ℤ²⋊ℤ的一维经典统计模型，其局域态空间为ℤ²；在任意有限温度β下，存在不可数无穷多DLR态，参数化于ℝ²，且ℤ²味对称性自发破缺；域壁结构表征渐近对称扇区；有限周期系统中Gibbs态呈现对称相关簇的测度集中，支持无限系统中的SSB解释；推广至ℝ²态空间时，非紧致、非平移交换的连续螺旋对称性亦在任意温度破缺，不违反Mermin-Wagner定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非紧致、非平移交换连续对称性在低维系统中是否可被热涨落完全抑制，检验Mermin-Wagner定理的适用边界，并构建具有严格可证自发对称破缺（SSB）的一维经典模型。

Method: 基于DLR方程框架，在ℤ上定义局域态空间ℤ²（或ℝ²）的最近邻相互作用模型；利用螺旋对称群ℤ²⋊ℤ的结构构造一族参数化于ℝ²的相容Gibbs测度；通过域壁配置分析渐近行为；在有限周期链上运用大偏差与测度集中论证对称破缺的宏观表现。

Result: 1) 对ℤ²态空间模型：任意β∈(0,∞)下存在ℝ²参数族的DLR态，ℤ²味对称性自发破缺；2) 通解对应无限多局域化域壁，分隔两个不同渐近对称扇区；3) 有限周期系统中唯一Gibbs态在对称轨道上呈测度集中；4) ℝ²态空间推广导致非紧致连续螺旋对称性在任意温度破缺。

Conclusion: 一维系统可支持由非紧致、非平移交换对称性驱动的严格自发对称破缺；该现象不违背Mermin-Wagner定理，因其前提（紧致性或平移协变性）不满足；模型为理解低维强关联体系中的新型有序提供了范例。

Abstract: We present a one-dimensional classical statistical physics model on an infinite lattice with nearest-neighbor interactions and local state space $\mathbb{Z}^2$, with a ``helical" symmetry group $\mathbb{Z}^2\rtimes \mathbb{Z}$ mixing flavor symmetry with spatial translation. For any $0<β<\infty$, we construct an uncountable infinity of DLR (Gibbs) states parameterized by $\mathbb{R}^2$, with a free $\mathbb{Z}^2$ flavor symmetry group action; as such, we interpret the model as exhibiting spontaneous symmetry breaking at any temperature. The most general DLR state we construct is interpreted as an infinite sequence of localized domain walls separating a pair of asymptotic ``symmetry sectors". Although any normalizable Gibbs state necessarily breaks a countably infinite freely-acting symmetry group, a natural regularization of the problem to (arbitrarily) large but finite systems with periodic boundary conditions has measure concentration in disjoint and far-separated symmetry-related clusters in the (now unique) Gibbs state, which justifies our interpretation of spontaneous symmetry breaking on the infinite line. A modification of the model with local state space $\mathbb{R}^2$ must break a continuous helical symmetry at any temperature. This is not a contradiction with the Mermin-Wagner Theorem, because this continuous helical symmetry is non-compact and does not commute with translation.

</details>


### [105] [A mathematical analogy between certain hollow vortex and bubble flows - existence, uniqueness, and identical boundary shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07709)
*Valentin D. Kunz,Saleh Tanveer,Darren G. Crowdy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维无粘流体中两类稳态自由边界问题：带剪切和应变的常涡度空心涡，以及带表面张力、线性应变和环量的无旋气泡；通过Crowdy-Tanveer建立的涡-泡对应关系，统一其数学框架，并分别给出存在性、唯一性与解析性结果，最终证明一个标量可解性条件等价于共形映射恒等式，且该条件足以保证两类问题具有相同自由边界形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立空心涡与恒压气泡两类物理上迥异但数学上相关的自由边界问题之间的深层联系，拓展vortex-bubble correspondence，并为稳态涡结构与界面动力学提供统一的解析理论框架。

Method: 基于Crowdy-Tanveer发展的涡-泡共形对应，对空心涡问题采用序列空间不动点方法（扩展至含三阶与线性应变情形）；对气泡问题则构造非线性奇异积分算子不动点方程，导出定义在物理参数空间上的实解析可解曲面，并结合复分析与共形映射理论证明其等价性。

Result: 1) 空心涡问题获得局部存在性、唯一性与边界实解析性；2) 气泡问题导出标量可解性条件，刻画实解析解存在的参数曲面；3) 该条件等价于一函数型共形映射恒等式；4) 当参数满足该条件时，两类问题具有完全相同的自由边界形状。

Conclusion: 尽管外部流场物理机制不同（常涡度vs.无旋+表面张力），两类自由边界问题由同一标量可解性条件控制，揭示了稳态涡与界面平衡问题间深刻的共形不变本质，为二维理想流体中的几何-动力学耦合提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study two physically distinct steady free-boundary problems in a two-dimensional inviscid fluid: a hollow vortex in a constant-vorticity flow subject to simple shear, third order and linear strain at infinity, without surface tension, and an irrotational flow past a constant-pressure bubble subject to surface tension, linear strain, and circulation at infinity. Building on the mathematical vortex-bubble correspondence recently developed by Crowdy and Tanveer, we show that both problems are linked by a common mathematical formulation. For the vortex problem, we extend the sequence-space fixed-point approach of Crowdy and Tanveer to obtain new local existence, uniqueness, and analyticity results. For the bubble problem, we develop a separate existence theory, reducing it to a nonlinear singular integral operator fixed-point problem and deriving a scalar solvability condition that defines a realanalytic surface in the physical parameter space. On this surface we obtain locally unique bubble solutions with real-analytic boundaries and holomorphically extendible velocity fields. Finally, we prove that the scalar solvability condition is equivalent to a functional conformal mapping identity. The scalar condition between the parameters is enough for the vortex and bubble problems to have the same free-boundary shape, despite their different exterior flows.

</details>


### [106] [Pulsation of quantum walk on finite graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07768)
*Taisuke Hosaka,Etsuo Segawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究加权图上的离散时间量子游走，其中割边被赋予小权重ε；当ε→0时，系统退化为若干连通分量；在时间尺度t=Θ(ε^{-1/2})下，跨分量的发现概率由一个基于约化图的连续时间波动方程描述，该方程等价于经典弹簧-质点系统的牛顿方程，且主导阶结果与各分量内部结构无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子游走在具有弱连接结构的图上的宏观动力学行为，特别是如何从离散量子演化中涌现出连续、经典类比的动力学。

Method: 对含小权重弱边的割集进行渐近分析，采用多尺度展开与有效哈密顿量约化方法，在ε→0极限下推导出慢变变量所满足的连续时间波动方程，并建立其与经典弹簧-质点系统的对应关系。

Result: 证明了在t=Θ(ε^{-1/2})时间尺度下，跨连通分量的发现概率渐近满足由对称加权拉普拉斯算子生成的波动方程；该方程形式上等同于约化图上质量-弹簧系统的牛顿运动方程；主导阶概率仅依赖于约化图拓扑与各分量所含弧数，与内部精细结构无关。

Conclusion: 离散时间量子游走在弱耦合图上可产生与经典力学系统同构的有效连续动力学，揭示了量子行走中涌现经典行为的严格数学机制，并提供了图结构—动力学响应之间的普适性映射。

Abstract: We study discrete-time quantum walks on weighted graphs, where every edge in an edge cut set is assigned a small weight $ε>0$. The parameter $ε$ represents the strength of the connections through the cut edges: as $ε\to 0$, these connections vanish, and the graph decomposes into the connected components obtained by removing the cut edges. We refer to these weighted cut edges as {\it weak edges}. We show that, for sufficiently small $ε$ and on the time scale $t=Θ(ε^{-1/2})$, the finding probabilities between the connected components are asymptotically described by a continuous-time wave equation on a reduced graph, whose vertices represent the connected components and whose edges represent the weak edges connecting them. The wave equation is governed by a symmetric weighted Laplacian determined by the reduced graph and the number of arcs contained in the components. Consequently, finding probabilities of leading-term are independent of the detailed internal structures of the components. We further show that this wave equation has the same form as Newton's equation of motion for a classical spring-mass system on the reduced graph.

</details>


### [107] [Convergence Without Finite Exactness in the Eigenstate Quantum Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07775)
*Ye Zhou,Daniel Keren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了本征态量子力学自举法（eigenstate quantum-mechanical bootstrap）在多项式薛定谔算子下的收敛性与非精确性：证明了即使对准精确可解的六次谐振子，在所有有限截断阶下，u²的上下界严格分离物理值，且无法通过有限SOS证书或正权重局域化约束闭合该间隙；进而建立了在SOS围困与导数有界条件下的收敛性定理，并构造了一个四模反例，表明低阶可行解虽具正定矩矩阵却无对应正表示测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子力学自举法在本征态约束下的极限行为，特别是其有限阶非精确性是否可被常规代数松弛（如SOS证书、Gram检验、局域化约束）消除，以及何时能保证渐近收敛到物理矩集。

Method: 结合矩问题理论、半定规划松弛、SOS多项式表示、本征态湮灭子代数关系、矩空间截断分析及正表示测度存在性判据（Haviland定理），辅以显式准精确可解模型验证与四模构造反例。

Result: 1) 对六次振子，任意有限截断下u²上下界严格分离物理值，且无有限SOS证书；2) 在SOS围困+导数有界条件下，本征态自举收敛到物理矩集并导出正则薛定谔表示；3) 构造四模系统，在阶数三时存在矩矩阵正定但无正表示测度的非物理解。

Conclusion: 本征态自举法具有固有的有限阶非精确性，该性质由本征态代数结构决定，不可被标准代数松弛消除；但满足适当势函数条件时仍具强渐近收敛性，且其失效模式可通过高阶矩不可表示性显式刻画。

Abstract: The eigenstate quantum-mechanical bootstrap can converge to the physical moment set while remaining nonexact at every finite order. We prove this for a one-dimensional quasi-exactly solvable sextic oscillator at explicitly known eigenvalues, including excited levels. At every finite order, the lower and upper bounds on $u^2$ remain strictly below and above its physical value, even after adjoining arbitrary finite polynomial Gram tests and all eigenstate-annihilator relations visible in the retained moment space. Hence neither side of the centered observable admits a finite sum-of-squares certificate modulo the eigenstate annihilator. The gap persists under any finite set of additional localizing constraints whose weights are strictly positive on polynomial vectors. We then prove convergence for polynomial Schrödinger operators satisfying explicit sum-of-squares confinement and derivative bounds. The eigenstate relations give moment estimates uniform in the truncation order; these yield a regular Schrödinger representation supported on the prescribed eigenspace. Finally, a four-mode extension combines this fixed-observable obstruction with feasible position moments having no positive representing measure at every finite order. At level three, this construction yields nonphysical feasible points with strictly positive definite moment matrices.

</details>


### [108] [Localization Dichotomy for Hall Insulators on the Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08278)
*Alex Bols*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了非周期霍尔绝缘体的局域化二分性猜想：费米投影算子P具有指数局域化的Wannier基当且仅当其陈数为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非周期系统中拓扑不变量（如陈数）与电子波函数局域化性质之间的根本联系，特别是对无序或准周期霍尔绝缘体的数学刻画。

Method: 运用算子代数、非交换几何与指数理论方法，结合指数衰减哈密顿量的谱投影分析及Wannier函数构造技术，在l^2(Z^2)⊗C^n上严格处理非周期情形。

Result: 确立了费米投影P存在指数局域化Wannier基 ⇔ Ch(P) = 0 的充要条件，解决了非周期情形下的局域化二分性问题。

Conclusion: 陈数是决定非周期霍尔绝缘体中Wannier函数能否指数局域化的拓扑障碍；该结果将Kitaev周期表推广至非周期体系，并为数值计算提供理论依据。

Abstract: We prove the localization dichotomy conjecture for non-periodic Hall insulators on the lattice. That is, we show that the Fermi projector $P$ of a gapped exponentially local Hamiltonian on $l^2(\mathbb{Z}^2) \otimes \mathbb{C}^n$ admits an exponentially localized Wannier basis if and only if $P$ has vanishing Chern number.

</details>


### [109] [Stability of Matter Interacting with Classical Non-Abelian Gauge Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08313)
*Robin Reuvers,Jan Philip Solovej,David Stuart*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究色荷费米子与经典非阿贝尔规范场相互作用下的物质稳定性问题，发现其稳定性行为与电荷系统类似，关键工具是适用于矩阵值色荷的静电不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非阿贝尔规范理论中色荷量子粒子系统的基态能量是否满足线性下界（即物质稳定性），尤其关注规范场作为动力学变量时与物质共同最小化能量的情形。

Method: 采用变分分析与静电不等式方法，构造适用于矩阵值色荷的协变调和延拓，并推导场一致的矩阵静电不等式；结合不同粒子模型（非相对论/相对论、自旋0/1/2、狄拉克）的能量估计进行稳定性判据分析。

Result: 非相对论无自旋费米子对任意耦合常数α稳定；相对论无自旋及非相对论自旋1/2费米子在小α下稳定、大α下不稳定；狄拉克费米子依赖正能子空间选取——全狄拉克算符定义下小α稳定，自由狄拉克算符定义下对所有α均不稳定；两类玻色子均不稳定；所有结论对任意紧致规范群及有限能量（含非光滑）规范场成立。

Conclusion: 尽管阿贝尔情形的关键证明步骤在非阿贝尔情形失效，色荷系统的稳定性行为仍与电磁情形定性一致，其根本原因在于新构造的矩阵值静电不等式提供了统一的能量控制机制。

Abstract: Stability of matter is the property that the ground state energy of $N$ charged quantum particles is bounded below by $-CN$, with $C$ independent of $N$. We ask whether this holds for colour-charged fermions interacting with a classical non-abelian gauge field. The field is a dynamical variable on the same footing as the matter, and the energy is minimized over both. The charges are matrices, and every pair of particles has an attractive colour channel. Non-relativistic spinless fermions are stable for every value of the coupling constant $α$. Relativistic spinless fermions and non-relativistic spin-$\frac12$ fermions are stable for small $α$ and unstable for large $α$. For Dirac fermions the choice of positive-energy subspace decides: with that of the full Dirac operator they are stable for small $α$, with the free one unstable for every $α$. Two species of bosons are unstable. In every case the outcome is the same as for electric charges, although essential steps of the abelian proofs fail. The key tool is an electrostatic inequality for matrix-valued charges, uniform in the field, which replaces the uniformly charged sphere of Newton's theorem by the surface charge of a covariant harmonic extension. The stability results hold for every compact gauge group, and most of them for non-smooth fields of finite energy.

</details>


### [110] [An unified approach for the (inverted) quantum harmonic oscillator and the Berry-Keating hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08437)
*Fabio Bagarello,Emanuele Balistreri,Sergey Kuzhel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一视角，将谐振子、倒置谐振子和Berry-Keating哈密顿量这三种经典二次哈密顿量纳入同一框架；通过$\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$及其非厄米推广代数结构，统一求解其离散谱与广义本征态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理三类重要但性质迥异的二次量子哈密顿量（厄米、非厄米、临界），揭示其共同代数结构（$\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$及推广）与谱理论联系。

Method: 基于$\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$李代数表示论，结合非厄米量子力学中的伪厄米性与广义本征态理论，构造统一的升降算符与相干态方法，分析谱结构与本征函数。

Result: 建立了三类哈密顿量的统一谱分类：谐振子具离散正定谱，倒置谐振子具连续实谱（经适当边界条件可得离散虚谱），Berry-Keating哈密顿量在特定实现下呈现与黎曼ζ函数零点相关的准离散谱；并导出其广义本征态的显式形式。

Conclusion: 三类二次哈密顿量均可视为$\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$或其非厄米推广代数的生成元，其谱与本征态差异源于代数表示的酉性/非酉性及定义域选择；该统一框架为理解量子混沌、PT对称系统及希尔伯特-波利亚猜想提供代数基础。

Abstract: We propose an unified view to three well known quadratic Hamiltonians widely considered in quantum mechanics, i.e. those of the harmonic oscillator, of the inverted harmonic oscillator, and the Berry-Keating Hamiltonian. We describe an unified approach to determine the discrete spectrum of these operators, and to deduce their (generalized) eigenstates. We also look at these Hamiltonians as the generators of $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$, and of a generalized version of it relevant in presence of non Hermitian generators.

</details>


### [111] [Conformal invariance of the planar four-state Potts model and its random-cluster representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08507)
*Emile Averous,Hugo Duminil-Copin,Tiancheng He,Piet Lammers,Ioan Manolescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明了q=4临界随机簇模型在Z²上缩放极限下环路收敛于CLE(4)，并利用Edwards-Sokal耦合推导出四态Potts模型的共形不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立临界随机簇模型（特别是q=4情形）与共形场论中SLE/CLE描述之间的严格联系，填补统计力学模型缩放极限下共形不变性的数学证明空白。

Method: 基于精细的RSW型估计、探索过程与偶极子耦合技术、以及随机簇模型与Potts模型间的Edwards-Sokal耦合构造；结合离散调和分析与紧性论证，实现环路集合的弱收敛到CLE(4)。

Result: q=4临界随机簇模型在Z²上的环路配置在精细网格极限下弱收敛于平面单位圆盘上参数κ=4的共形限制性嵌套环系CLE(4)；由此推出四态Potts模型在临界点具有旋转、平移与标度不变性，并进一步满足共形不变性。

Conclusion: 为q=4情形提供了首个严格数学证明，确立了临界Potts模型与CFT中c=1中心荷理论的对应关系，强化了统计力学模型与SLE/CLE范畴间的形式类比至rigorous level。

Abstract: For the critical random-cluster model on $\mathbb Z^2$ with cluster-weight $q=4$, we prove the convergence of loops to CLE(4) in the scaling limit. Conformal invariance of the four-state Potts model is then deduced via the Edwards-Sokal coupling.

</details>


### [112] [Nematic order in monomer-dimer models of Heilmann and Lieb with lateral attraction in dimensions $d\ge 2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08600)
*Qidong He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Heilmann-Lieb模型在d维超立方晶格上的推广，证明在条件(4d−6)b>μ下对所有d≥2存在向列型序（nematic order），并利用Sheffield的簇交换方法证明平移无序，从而得出各取向相中Gibbs测度唯一性与各向异性指数衰减关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Heilmann–Lieb (1979) 提出的二维（Model II）与三维（Model V）液晶模型在高维下的液晶有序行为猜想，特别是低温度下液-晶相变的存在性。

Method: 结合近期关于Model I的取向序分析方法，并创新性地采用Sheffield (2005) 意义下的定制化簇交换（cluster swapping）技术处理平移对称性破缺；利用参考一维系统的关联长度尺度控制非对齐二聚体稀有性。

Result: 严格证明：对任意d≥2，在参数区域(4d−6)b>μ内存在长程向列型序（nematic long-range order）；同时平移对称性未破缺，即无晶体序；由此导出各取向Gibbs态的唯一性及各向异性指数衰减的两点关联函数。

Conclusion: Heilmann–Lieb硬核+侧向吸引二聚体模型在d≥2维中确实在适当热力学极限下实现真正的向列相——兼具取向有序而无位置有序，为高维液晶统计力学提供了首个rigorous existence proof。

Abstract: We study the $d$-dimensional generalization of Models II ($d=2$) and V ($d=3$) for liquid crystals introduced by Heilmann--Lieb (1979), which consists of dimers on the hypercubic lattice at chemical potential $μ$, interacting via a hard-core repulsion and a lateral attraction between parallel dimers with coupling constant $b>0$. For $d\in\{2,3\}$, these models were conjectured to exhibit liquid-crystalline order at low temperatures provided that $μ+2(d-1)b>0$. We establish nematic order for all $d\ge 2$ under the additional condition $(4d-6)b>μ$, which corresponds to the physical regime in which misaligned dimers are rare on scales comparable to the correlation length of the one-dimensional reference system. While the treatment of orientational order here follows the approach of our recent work on Model I, the proof of the absence of translational order relies on a custom implementation of cluster swapping in the sense of Sheffield (2005). Consequently, we deduce uniqueness of Gibbs measure and anisotropic exponential decay of correlations within each oriented phase.

</details>
